# 100集原稿与逐集核对意见

日期：2026-11-11（北京时间）。范围：001–100集，100份JSON、100份Markdown、900个分镜。

下列口播、镜名、镜位和绘图提示词从JSON逐项导出；编辑意见单独列出，**没有写回源剧本，也没有批准任何改编方案**。全部900段口播与现有Markdown核对一致。原稿中的运动字段属于旧建议，执行时仍服从当前固定画面要求。

核对方式：每次确定一集的故事核心、主场景、角色目标、小事件与结尾，再决定保留、修改或换题。相近集数表示本次编辑识别的可比对象，不是机器相似度评分。所有新地点、任务和角色安排均为讨论候选，未制作配音或图片。


## 第001集

**原题：小兔子皮皮的晚安星光**

原主题：在熟悉的陪伴里放松与入睡。原场景：已验收的小木屋卧室及窗外。原客串：无。

**处理建议：保留为单集入睡特例（待用户逐集核对）。**

问题与证据：第1镜已经靠枕盖毯，第2/5/7镜看月星树叶；适合作为一次陪睡开篇，缺少事件，不宜成为100集通用模板。

可讨论的方向：保留这一集的卧室陪伴功能；后续集切换地点、人物目标与结尾，不沿用它的床窗构图。

优先对照：002、007、086、090、097、100。

来源：[JSON快照](原稿快照/02_100集剧本/结构化JSON/ep001.json) · [Markdown快照](原稿快照/02_100集剧本/单集Markdown/第001集_小兔子皮皮的晚安星光.md)。

### 原稿九镜

#### 镜01｜小屋与小灯

夜深了，森林里的小屋，亮着一盏暖暖的小灯。
小兔子皮皮靠在枕头上，盖着他的小毛毯。

镜位：中景建立环境，主体位置清楚

原绘图提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Asset type: the first full-bleed vertical 9:16 frame and visual reference for a gentle preschool bedtime story, no panels. Create a beautiful refined hand-painted watercolor children's picture-book illustration with visible subtle paper grain, rounded simple shapes and soft edges, muted dusty blue, sage and cream, warm restrained amber light, peaceful quiet atmosphere. Scene: inside a small cozy wooden forest cottage bedroom at night. Composition: small wooden bed on the left/lower middle, wooden window in the upper right showing a crescent moon and a dark quiet leafy forest. A little shaded bedside lamp casts soft warm light. Main character Pip is a small adorable soft gray bunny, round face, tiny pink nose, long relaxed ears, wearing a deep navy drooping nightcap dotted with a few tiny pale yellow stars. Pip rests comfortably against a cream pillow with sleepy half-open eyes, a warm yellow blanket pulled over his body. A small simple orange carrot plush with muted green leaves rests near his paws. Keep the room uncluttered, use confident storybook composition, gentle contrast without harsh highlights, hand-drawn facial features, not photorealistic and not 3D. The bunny should be clearly readable at phone size. Leave a quiet low-detail area near the lower edge for possible later captions. Full-bleed single image, absolutely no text, letters, labels, borders, collages or watermark. Portrait 9:16, ideally 1080x1920 or larger.

#### 镜02｜月亮与树叶

窗外，月亮弯弯的，树叶轻轻摇。

镜位：主体或物件近景，保留地点线索

原绘图提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Asset: one new full-bleed 9:16 vertical illustration for scene 02 of the SAME gentle bedtime picture book. Reference image 1 supplies the established cottage bedroom, watercolor technique, wooden window, cream curtains printed with muted olive leaves, deep blue forest, warm amber and dusty blue palette. Create a closer view of this SAME wooden crossbar window seen from inside the room. The crescent moon is visible near the upper right, quiet blue forest and a few soft leafy branches outside. Curtains frame both sides. A very small edge of the cream pillow and yellow blanket may appear at lower left, but NO bunny is required. Focus on the calm moon and softly resting leaves to match the narration about moon and leaves. The special golden star has not appeared yet: do not add a prominent five-point star. Soft warm lamplight touches the lower window sill. Exact same refined hand-painted watercolor and paper grain as reference, muted colors and gentle contrast, no realism, no 3D, no new props. Single image, no panels, text, border, labels or watermark. Portrait 9:16.

#### 镜03｜哈欠与放松

皮皮揉揉眼睛，打了个小小的哈欠。
他把长耳朵轻轻放好，小爪子也放松了。

镜位：中近景呈现动作后的稳定姿态

原绘图提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Input image 1 is the approved visual reference for character, room, watercolor technique and palette. Create ONE new full-bleed portrait 9:16 shot for scene 02 of the same bedtime picture book. Preserve precisely the gray bunny Pip's round face, pink nose, long soft floppy ears, deep navy drooping nightcap with pale yellow stars, warm yellow blanket, cream pillow, cute orange carrot plush with green leaves, wooden bed, leafy curtains, warm lamp and blue night window. Change composition to a closer medium shot of Pip from chest up in the same bed. His eyes are half closed and his little mouth forms a gentle tiny yawn, relaxed and adorable, no dramatic expression. His paws rest on the blanket pulled up to his chin, carrot plush beside one paw. Do not add hands or limbs. Keep enough of the window in the upper right and warm bedside light from lower left to recognize the same room. Muted peaceful soft hand-painted watercolor and paper texture, no dramatic contrast, no photorealism, no 3D. Single illustration, not a collage, no text, no borders, no watermark. Portrait 9:16.

#### 镜04｜软软的毛毯

小毛毯软软的，一直盖到下巴。

镜位：细节特写，避免裁掉必要的爪子或道具

原绘图提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Reference image 1 defines the same gray bunny Pip, navy star nightcap, yellow blanket, carrot plush, wooden bed and watercolor style. Generate ONE new full-bleed vertical 9:16 bedtime story illustration, a detail close-up to accompany 'the little blanket is soft, pulled up to his chin'. Frame the bunny's lower round face and tiny pink nose near the upper third, long floppy ears beside his cheeks, two relaxed soft gray paws resting on the edge of the SAME warm yellow blanket tucked up under his chin. Show the cozy blanket filling the lower half with beautiful gentle watercolor texture. The same small orange carrot plush with muted green leaves peeks beside one paw, exactly one toy, no extra limbs. A little part of the navy cap with pale yellow stars is visible at the top. Cream pillow behind him; the warm wooden bedroom and a tiny hint of the blue night window remain in the background. #### 镜05｜向星星道晚安

窗边的一颗小星星，慢慢亮了起来。
皮皮望着它，在心里轻轻说：小星星，晚安。
小星星安安静静地，陪着他。

原绘图提示词：Use case: illustration-story. Input image 1 is the established visual reference for Pip, the bedroom and the watercolor palette. Generate one new single full-bleed portrait 9:16 illustration: scene 03, looking toward the SAME wooden bedroom window from the side of the bunny's bed. The window fills the upper two-thirds, its wooden crossbars, cream leafy curtains and deep blue forest are consistent with the reference. The same small crescent moon remains near the upper right. Add one modest softly glowing five-point pale golden star just to the LEFT of that crescent moon; it is small and gentle, no flare, no trail. In the lower left foreground show only the back and a little side profile of the SAME sleepy gray bunny with the navy yellow-star nightcap and long floppy ear, looking up toward the star while still tucked under the yellow blanket, not standing. A little warm light from the bedside lamp below the frame. Preserve the hand-painted watercolor paper texture, subdued cozy amber/blue palette, simple rounded shapes. 镜位：安顿动作中景或安静环境近景

Preserve his soft gray fur, round face, tiny pink triangular nose, long floppy ears and deep navy nightcap with pale yellow stars and yellow pom-pom. His eyes are fully closed with relaxed curved eyelids and a tiny calm smile. The same warm yellow blanket rests below his chin and the same orange carrot plush with muted green leaves is tucked beside one paw near the lower edge. Use a slightly higher bedside angle than reference, concentrating on the face, cap and soft pillow, rather than repeating a full-room view. A tiny softly painted hint of the wooden bedroom and blue night window in the upper-right background, no extra furniture. Slightly dimmer muted amber and soft night-blue shadows, matching watercolor pigments and paper grain, no 3D, no realistic fur, no bright glow. Absolutely no text, border, collage or watermark. Portrait 9:16.

The SAME gray bunny is very small and comfortably asleep in the bed, eyes fully closed, navy yellow-star nightcap, long floppy ears, warm yellow blanket, hugging the orange carrot plush. Preserve the curved wooden headboard and footboard and cream pillow, same bed/window arrangement and slanted wooden ceiling beam. Moon and a modest pale gold star immediately to its left show outside the upper-right window. Let the room be noticeably dimmer and more blue-grey than the first scene, while keeping a little soft amber lamp glow and readable warm blanket. Broad calm low-detail negative space in the lower third, no new furniture or props. Refined hand-painted watercolor with visible soft paper grain and low saturation, gentle storybook shapes, peaceful composition. ## 第002集

**原题：皮皮的月光小口袋**

原主题：陪伴不必握在手里。原场景：小木屋卧室。原客串：无。

**处理建议：优先重构（待用户逐集核对）。**

问题与证据：第1–8镜都围绕床上月光、抚被和玩偶，想收集光又放下，与001的星光陪伴接近。

可讨论的方向：可改为皮皮和团团在林间散步时想收藏不同声音，最后用声音辨认回程；让行动和发现推进，结尾停在林间告别处。

优先对照：001、022、040、090、098。

来源：[JSON快照](原稿快照/02_100集剧本/结构化JSON/ep002.json) · [Markdown快照](原稿快照/02_100集剧本/单集Markdown/第002集_皮皮的月光小口袋.md)。

可讨论的方向：保留“找到适合自己的舒服”，改成朋友们在果园布置野餐座位，尝试不同坐法后各得其所；尾镜留在果树下。

## 第004集

**原题：被角的小山坡**

原主题：让小小的不平整安顿下来。原场景：小木屋卧室。原客串：无。

**处理建议：优先重构（待用户逐集核对）。**

问题与证据：毛毯鼓起、伸脚、展开被角、抱玩偶再闭眼，只是003“调整寝具”的换物版本。

可讨论的方向：换成真实草坡上的小任务：把滚到坡脚的野餐篮稳稳安放，发现平地适合分享点心；避免只把被角改名山坡。

## 第061集

**原题：小画笔洗好澡**

原主题：为创作留下柔和的收尾。原场景：小木屋画桌与卧室。原客串：无。

**处理建议：保留故事核后重写（待用户逐集核对）。**

问题与证据：洗笔、吸水、放架是工具护理流程，后半盖毯停工；与018收针线、065收鼓同构。

可讨论的方向：可写公共画室里朋友互换工具作画，结束时一起清洗和辨认各自的笔，留下共同作品；护理只是事件收尾。
